{D47}]'phags pa dkon mchog brtsegs pa chen po'i chos kyi rnam grangs stong phrag brgya pa las/_le'u gsum pa ste/_lag na rdo rjes zhus pa zhes bya ba de bzhin gshegs pa'i gsang ba bsam gyis mi khyab pa bstan pa/_bam po dang po/_/
[\[100a.2\]]rgya gar skad du/_Ar+ya ta thA ga ta a tsin t+ya gu h+ya nir de sha nA ma ma hA yA na sU tra/_bod skad du/_'phags pa de bzhin gshegs pa'i gsang ba bsam gyis mi khyab pa bstan pa zhes bya ba theg pa chen po'i mdo/_/bam po dang po/_sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' 
[\[100a.3\]]thams cad la phyag 'tshal lo/_/'di skad bdag gis thos pa'i dus gcig na/_bcom ldan 'das rgyal po'i khab na/_bya rgod kyi phung po'i ri la dge slong bzhi khri nyis stong gi dge slong gi dge 'dun chen po dang /_byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po mngon par 
[\[100a.4\]]shes pa mngon par shes pa phal cher sangs rgyas kyi zhing gzhan nas 'dus pa'i byang chub sems dpa' brgyad khri bzhi stong dang thabs cig tu bzhugs te/_thams cad kyang bzod pa thob pa/_phyir mi ldog pa/_skye ba gcig gis thogs pa/_gzungs thob pa/_ting nge 'dzin 
[\[100a.5\]]thob pa/_spobs pa thogs pa med pa/_phyogs bcu mtha' yas pa'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing du 'gro ba la mkhas pa/_mngon par shes pa'i ye shes kyis rnam par rtsen pa/_phas kyi rgol ba thams cad kyi zil gyis mi non pa/_bdud dang phyir rgol ba bcom pa/_sems can thams cad 
[\[100a.6\]]kyi sems dang bsam pa dang spyod pa'i bsgrub pa shes pa/_dbang po mchog dang mchog ma yin pa shes pa la mkhas pa/_pha rol tu phyin pa thams cad kyi tshul la 'jug par rab tu zhugs pa/_thabs la mkhas pa'i dam pa'i pha rol tu son pa/_sangs rgyas thams cad kyis 
[\[100a.7\]]bstod cing bsngags te/_bkur la rab tu bsngags pa/_bskal pa bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong dpag tu med par byang chub kyi lam mtha' yas pa mthar gtugs pa/_sems sa dang /_chu dang /_me dang /_rlung dang mtshungs pa/_nam mkha' ltar mtha' yas pa'i 
[\[100b\]]
[\[100b.1\]]byams pa bsgom pa'i rnal 'byor la brtson pa/_chags pa'i gnas sgrib pa dang /_chod pa dang /_kun nas ldang ba thams cad las yang dag par 'das pa/_mthu bo che'i lus thob pa/_rdo rje ltar sra zhing mi tshugs pa'i lus dang ldan pa/_seng ge'i sgra chen po sgrogs 
[\[100b.2\]]pa/_'khor thams cad zil gyis gnon pa la 'jigs pa med par mkhas pa/_zla ba dang nyi ma mog mog por byed pa/_chos thams cad ji lta ba bzhin du dus gsum mnyam pa nyid thob pa/_chos zab mo rnam par nges pa/_ye shes kyis ston pa la mkhas pa/_rten cing 
[\[100b.3\]]'brel par 'byung ba la zhugs pas rtag pa dang chad par lta ba'i mtha' gnyis yongs su spangs pa/_bsam gtan dang /_rnam par thar pa dang /_ting nge 'dzin dang /_snyoms par 'jug pa dang /_mnyam par gzhag pa dang /_ldang ba shes pa la mkhas pa/_phyogs bcur 
[\[100b.4\]]sgra grags pa/_dam pa'i chos dkon mchog gi mdzod yang dag par 'dzin pa/_dkon mchog gsum gyi rigs rgyun mi 'chad par byed pa/_bsod nams dang ye shes kyi tshogs dpag tu med pa bsags pa sha stag la 'di lta ste/_byang chub sems dpa' zla ba'i bla ma zhes bya ba dang /_
[\[100b.5\]]zla ba'i tog dang /_ri bong can gyi tog dang /_'od kyi tog dang /_'od dpal dang /_dpal brtsegs dang /_dpal sbas dang /_mthu bo che dang /_klu dga' dang /_klu'i bla ma dang /_klus byin dang /_gzugs 'dzin dang /_mya ngam gyi lha dang /_yon tan sgron ma'i rgyal po dang /_lag na 
[\[100b.6\]]sgron ma dang /_rtag tu lag bteg dang /_rtag tu lag brkyang dang /_lag na phyag rgya rin po che dang /_lag na rin po che dang /_kun du snang dang /_skar ma'i rgyal po dang /_lag na rdo rje dang /_rdo rje'i blo gros dang /_rdo rje'i blo gros chen po dang /_rdo rjes rnam par gnon dang /_mi g.yo ba'i gom par 
[\[100b.7\]]'gro ba dang /_'jig rten gsum rnam par gnon dang /_mtha' yas blo gros dang /_blo gros mtha' yas dang /_blo gros rgya mtsho dang /_brtan pa'i blo gros dang /_nor gyi blo gros dang /_bla ma'i blo gros dang /_khyad par blo gros dang /_'phel ba'i blo gros dang /_rtag tu mos dang /_rtag tu dgod 
[\[101a\]]
[\[101a.1\]]cing rab tu dga' ba'i dbang po dang /_/ngan song spong dang /_sgrib pa thams cad rnam par sel ba dang /_shin tu rnam par sbyangs pa dri ma med pa'i gzi brjid 'chang ba dang /_brtson 'grus 'bar dang /_shes rab brtsegs dang /_rtag par lta dang /_spyan 
[\[101a.2\]]ras gzigs dbang phyug dang /_mthu chen thob dang /_lhun po brtsegs dang /_nam mkha'i mdzod dang /_mi 'dzums pa dang /_brjod med dang /_rin chen dam pa dang /_rin chen sems pa dang /_legs par rnam par sems pa dang /_don legs par rnam par bsams pa 
[\[101a.3\]]dang /_shin du nges pa'i blo gros dang /_gzungs kyi dbang phyug gi rgyal po dang /_sa 'dzin dang /_bkod pa'i rgyal po dang /_zhing yang dag par brgyan pa dang /_rin chen 'byung gnas dang /_phug sbas dang /_dbang po'i lha dang /_chu lha dang /_tshangs pa dra ba can dang /_byang chub 
[\[101a.4\]]sems dpa'i 'dra ba can gyi 'od ces bya ba dang /_lha'i cod pan dang /_lag [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]dang /_mig [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]dang /_spos kyi glang po dang /_bal glang spos kyi glang po che dang /_seng ge'i tog dang /_don grub blo gros dang /_kha lo sgyur dang /_zhi ba'i blo gros dang /_byams pa dang /_
[\[101a.5\]]'jam dpal gzhon nur gyur pa dang /_de dag la sogs pa byang chub sems dpa' brgyad khri bzhi stong dang /_gang dag stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams 'di na brgya byin dang /_tshangs pa dang /_'jig rten skyong ba dang /_de ma yin pa'i lha'i dbang po 'am/_klu'i dbang po 'am/_
[\[101a.6\]]gnod sbyin gyi dbang po 'am/_dri za'i dbang po 'am/_lha ma yin gyi dbang po'am/_nam mkha' lding gi dbang po 'am/_mi 'am ci'i dbang po 'am/_lto 'phye chen po'i dbang po dbang che ba/_dbang che bar grags pa yod pa de dag thams cad kyang 'khor de nyid du 'dus par gyur pa la 
[\[101a.7\]]'di lta ste/_klu'i rgyal po ma dros pa zhes bya ba dang /_klu'i rgyal po rgya mtsho dang /_chu lha dang /_gzi can dang /_'jog po dang /_gser mdog dang /_kha dog mtha' yas dang /_klu'i rgyal po mtshams [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]dang /_de dag dang gzhan yang klu'i rgyal po 'khor dang 
[\[101b\]]
[\[101b.1\]]bcas pa brgya stong phrag mang po bcom ldan 'das la blta ba dang /_phyag bya ba dang /_mchod pa dang /_bsnyen bkur bya ba dang /_chos mnyan pa'i phyir 'khor de nyid du 'dus par gyur to/_/lha ma yin gyi dbang po sgra gcan dang /_thags zangs ris dang /_lag [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]dang /_sa la 
[\[101b.2\]]dang /_ta la dang /_bde mchog dang /_rab sim dang /_che rab dang /_lha ma yin gyi dbang po gnod sbyin gzhan dang /_de dag dang /_gzhan yang lha ma yin gyi dbang po 'khor dang bcas pa brgya stong mang po bcom ldan 'das la blta ba dang /_phyag bya ba dang /_mchod pa dang /_
[\[101b.3\]]bsnyen bkur bya ba dang /_chos mnyan pa'i phyir 'khor de nyid du 'dus par gyur to/_/rgyal po ma skyes dgra yang btsun mo'i 'khor dang bcas/_rang gi gnyen dang /_'khor dang bcas te bcom ldan 'das la blta ba dang /_phyag bya ba dang /_mchod pa dang /_bsnyen bkur bya ba 
[\[101b.4\]]dang /_chos mnyan pa'i phyir 'khor de nyid du 'dus par gyur to/_/de ma yin pa'i gzhan yang 'khor bzhi po dge slong dang /_dge slong ma dang /_dge bsnyen dang /_dge bsnyen ma rnams kyang bcom ldan 'das la blta ba dang /_phyag bya ba dang /_mchod pa dang /_bsnyen 
[\[101b.5\]]bkur bya ba dang /_chos mnyan pa'i phyir 'khor de nyid du 'dus par gyur to/_/sa bla na spyod pa'i lha'i bu rnams dang /_'dod pa na spyod pa dang /_gzugs na spyod pa dang /_gnas gtsang ma'i ris kyi lha'i bu rnams kyang /_bcom ldan 'das la blta ba dang /_phyag bya ba dang /_
[\[101b.6\]]mchod pa dang /_bsnyen bkur bya ba dang /_chos mnyan pa'i phyir 'khor de nyid du 'dus par gyur to/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das 'khor brgya stong du mas yongs su bskor cing mdun gyis bltas te 'di ltar byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams kyi tshogs rnam par dag pa'i 
[\[101b.7\]]sgo bsdu ba yongs su bkang ba'i phyir chos ston to/_/'di lta ste/_byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi sbyin pa'i tshogs ni sems can yongs su smin par byed par 'gyur ro/_/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi tshul khrims kyi tshogs ni smon lam yongs su rdzogs par 'gyur ro/_/
[\[102a\]]
[\[102a.1\]]byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi bzod pa'i tshogs ni mtshan dang dpe byad bzang po yongs su rdzogs par 'gyur ro/_/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi brtson 'grus kyi tshogs ni sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad yongs su rdzogs par 'gyur ro/_/byang chub 
[\[102a.2\]]sems dpa' rnams kyi bsam gtan gyi tshogs ni cang shes kyi sems su 'gyur ro/_/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi shes rab kyi tshogs ni nyon mongs pa thams cad spong bar 'gyur ro/_/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi chos bstan pa'i tshogs ni spobs pa thogs pa med par 
[\[102a.3\]]'gyur ro/_/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi bsod nams kyi tshogs ni sems can thams cad nye bar 'tsho bar 'gyur ro/_/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi ye shes kyi tshogs ni thogs pa med pa'i ye shes su 'gyur ro/_/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi zhi gnas kyi tshogs 
[\[102a.4\]]ni sems las su rung bar 'gyur ro/_/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi lhag mthong gi tshogs ni the tsom dang bral bar 'gyur ro/_/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi byams pa'i tshogs ni zhe 'gras pa med pa'i sems su 'gyur ro/_/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi snying rje'i tshogs 
[\[102a.5\]]ni yongs su mi skyo ba'i sems su 'gyur ro/_/dga' ba'i tshogs ni chos kyi kun dga' la dga' zhing mos par 'gyur ro/_/btang snyoms kyi tshogs ni rjes su chags pa dang /_khong khro ba spong bar 'gyur ro/_/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi chos mnyan pa'i tshogs ni sgrib pa 
[\[102a.6\]]spong bar 'gyur ro/_/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi mngon par 'byung ba'i tshogs ni yongs su 'dzin pa thams cad 'dor bar 'gyur ro/_/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi dgon pa la gnas pa'i tshogs ni byas pa'i las chud mi za bar 'gyur ro/_/dran pa'i tshogs ni gzungs 
[\[102a.7\]]thob par 'gyur ro/_/blo gros kyi tshogs ni blo rab tu 'byed par 'gyur ro/_/rtogs pa'i tshogs ni don rtogs pa'i rjes su 'gro bar 'gyur ro/_/dran pa nye bar gzhag pa'i tshogs ni lus dang /_tshor ba dang /_sems dang /_chos la so sor rtog par 'gyur ro/_/
[\[102b\]]
[\[102b.1\]]yang dag pa'i spong ba'i tshogs ni mi dge ba'i chos thams cad spong ba dang /_dge ba'i chos thams cad yongs su rdzogs par 'gyur ro/_/rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa'i tshogs ni lus dang sems yang bar 'gyur ro/_/dbang po lnga'i tshogs ni dbang po mchog dang mchog ma yin pa shes 
[\[102b.2\]]par 'gyur ro/_/stobs lnga'i tshogs ni nyon mongs pa can thams cad kyis mi rdzi bar 'gyur ro/_/byang chub kyi yan lag gi tshogs ni chos thams cad ji lta ba bzhin du khong du chud par 'gyur ro/_/lam gyi tshogs ni lam ngan pa thams cad las yang dag par 'da' bar 'gyur ro/_/
[\[102b.3\]]bden pa'i tshogs ni mi 'khrugs pa'i chos nyid du 'gyur ro/_/so so yang dag par rig pa'i tshogs ni sems can thams cad kyi the tsom gcod par 'gyur ro/_/rton pa'i tshogs ni gzhan la rag ma las pa'i ye shes su 'gyur ro/_/dge ba'i bshes gnyen gyi tshogs ni 
[\[102b.4\]]yon tan thams cad skye ba'i sgor 'gyur ro/_/bsam pa'i tshogs ni 'jig rten thams cad la mi slu bar 'gyur ro/_/lhag pa'i bsam pa'i tshogs ni khyad par du 'gro bar 'gyur ro/_/sbyor ba'i tshogs ni rtsom pa thams cad nyams 'og tu chud par 'gyur ro/_/nang du 
[\[102b.5\]]yang dag 'jog gi tshogs ni ji skad thos pa'i chos chud mi gzon par 'gyur ro/_/bsdu ba'i dngos po'i tshogs ni sems can yongs su smin par byed par 'gyur ro/_/dam pa'i chos yongs su 'dzin pa'i tshogs ni dkon mchog gsum gyi rigs rgyun mi gcod par 'gyur ro/_/
[\[102b.6\]]bsngo ba la mkhas pa'i tshogs ni sangs rgyas kyi zhing yongs su dag par 'gyur ro/_/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi thabs la mkhas pa'i tshogs ni thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes da yongs su rdzogs par 'gyur ro/_/de ltar bcom ldan 'das kyis byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' 
[\[102b.7\]]chen po rnams la tshogs rnam par dag pa'i sgo bsdu ba zhes bya ba'i chos kyi rnam grangs rgyas par bshad do/_/tshogs rnam par dag pa'i sgo bsdu ba bstan pa 'di bshad pa'i tshe/_bcom ldan 'das kyi g.yas phyogs na gsang ba pa'i bdag po lag na rdo rje rdo rje thogs nas 'dug par gyur to/_
[\[103a\]]
[\[103a.1\]]de nas bcom ldan 'das la gsang ba pa'i bdag po lag na rdo rjes 'di skad ces gsol to/_/ji tsam du de bzhin gshegs pas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams kyi tshogs rnam par dag pa'i sgo bsdu ba zhes bgyi ba'i chos kyi rnam grangs 'di 
[\[103a.2\]]legs par gsungs pa ni bcom ldan 'das ngo mtshar lags so/_/yang bcom ldan 'das kyis ji skad bshad pa'i don bdag gis 'tshal ba ltar na/_bdag 'di snyam bgyid de/_bcom ldan 'das byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams kyi tshogs de dag thams cad 
[\[103a.3\]]ni bsod nams kyi tshogs dang /_ye shes kyi tshogs kyi nang du 'dus shing rjes su zhugs par 'tshal bar bgyi'o/_/de ci'i slad du zhe na/_bcom ldan 'das byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams kyi bsod nams kyi tshogs ni rnam par bsgrub pa thams cad rnam par

[\[103a.5\]]slad du zhe na/_bcom ldan 'das bsod nams kyi tshogs kyis ni byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi thabs la mkhas pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa yongs su rdzogs par bgyid do/_/ye shes kyi tshogs kyis ni shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa yongs su rdzogs par bgyid de/_byang chub sems

[\[103a.7\]]las phyir mi ldog par 'gyur te/_phyir mi ldog pa'i byang chub sems dpa' la ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams de bzhin gshegs pa'i gsang ba thams cad ston te mi 'chab bo/_/de nas byang chub sems dpa' zhi ba'i blo gros kyis gsang ba pa'i bdag po lag na rdo rje la 
[\[103b\]]
[\[103b.1\]]'di skad ces smras so/_/gsang ba pa'i bdag po khyod ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i zhabs 'bring ba nye ba na 'dug pa yin na gsang ba'i bdag po de bzhin gshegs pa'i gsang ba'i gnas gang nyan thos dang /_rang sangs rgyas thams cad kyis yang ma yin na/_de ma yin pa'i sems can

[\[104a\]]
[\[104a.1\]]'das sangs rgyas kyi mthu dang /_sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis byang chub sems dpa'i gsang ba rnams dang /_de bzhin gshegs pa'i gsang ba rnams la gang cung zad 'jug par bgyi ba tsam bstan par bgyi'o/_/de nas gsang ba pa'i bdag po lag

[\[104a.7\]]de bzhin gshegs pa'i gsang ba nyan pa la mos par gyur cing /_thams cad dang ldan pa'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor du lha'i me tog gis pus mo nub tsam du bkram par gyur pa de lta bu'i byin gyi rlabs kyis byin gyis brlabs so/_/de nas gsang ba pa'i bdag po lag na rdo rjes byang chub 
[\[104b\]]
[\[104b.1\]]sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po zhi ba'i blo gros la 'di skad ces smras so/_/zhi ba'i blo gros de'i phyir shin tu legs par nyon la yid la zung shig dang /_byang chub sems dpa'i gsang ba'i gnas rnams bshad par bya'o/_/zhi ba'i blo gros gang tshun cad bcom ldan 'das

[\[150a.1\]]legs par thams cad lhan cig 'dus nas ni/_/sa yi gzhi ni legs par brgyan par bya/_/stong gsum brgyan par gyur la ltos shig kye/_/'dod pa'i khams kyang brgyan par gyur pa ltos/_/'dod pa'i khams ni ji ltar brgyan pa bzhin/_/de ltar 
[\[150a.2\]]gzugs kyi khams kyang brgyan par bya/_/tshangs pa rnams ni sems dang legs so zhes/_/rab tu dga' nas stong gsum brgyan par gyur/_/ji ltar 'dod pa'i khams na bkod snang ba/_/de bas lhag pa'i mchod pa de dag bstan/_/me tog rnams kyis bkang zhing 
[\[150a.3\]]spos kyis bkang /_/rin chen rnams kyis mchod cing sil snyan bsgrags/_/tshangs pa la sogs 'og min lha yi rnams/_/rab tu dga' nas stong gsum thams cad kyang /_/dga' bar 'gyur dang phyag bya'i 'os su byas/_/rang rang gnas kyi gzhi yang bkod pa byas/_/
[\[150a.4\]]lha rnams kyis ni gong nas gong du yang /_/lhag pa lhag pa'i rgyan rnams rab tu bstan/_/lha yi bu mo bye ba drug cu brgyas/_/byang chub rdo rje gdan ni rgyan par byed/_/spos chu yis ni kun tu chag chag 'debs/_/me tog sna tshogs dag kyang 'thor bar byed/_/sa gzhi dag 
[\[150a.5\]]la rin chen rnams kyang bkram/_/byang chub snying por rgyan rnams rab tu bstan/_/thams cad tshig gis brjod par mi nus pa/_/de snyed tshad kyi bkod pa 'dir byas gyur/_/zhi ba'i blo gros byang chub sems dpa' de byang chub kyi snying por 'gro ba na stong gi dbang po tshangs pa chen po 
[\[150a.6\]]mi mjed kyi bdag pos tshangs ris kyi lha thams cad la smras pa/_grogs po dag bskal pa bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong mang por brtul zhugs dang /_dka' thub rnams kyi spyod pa spyad pa sems can gyi mchog skyes bu chen po 'di byang chub kyi snying por bzhud kyis/_de la khyed kyis stong 
[\[150a.7\]]gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad bkod pa chen po rnams kyis brgyan par gyis shig_/de nas mi mjed kyi bdag po tshangs pas tshangs ris kyi lha'i bu rnams la tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so/_/sems can rnam dag brtul zhugs chos des pa/_/phan bzhed

[\[150b.1\]]'jig rten don mdzad bdag nyid che/_/de ni blo ngan sder bcas bdud 'dul du/_/shing gi dbang po byang chub drung du bzhud/_/thams cad 'dus te lhan cig 'dong bar bya/_/stong gsum mi mjed 'di ni brgyan par bya/_/'di ni lhar bcas 'jig rten chos kyi dbang /_/yang dag 
[\[150b.2\]]mchod na bsod nams mtha' yas 'gyur/_/tshangs pa de dag tshig mchog de thos nas/_/stong gsum thams cad ma lus rgyan byed cing /_/legs par phyags te me tog rgyal mtshan brgyan/_/'jig rten khams ni rin chen rang bzhin sprul/_/nyi ma zla ba'i 'od kyi

[\[150b.4\]]chen gzhi la gser gyi pad+ma bkram/_/kha khyer rim pa bdun gyis yongs su bskor/_/brgya byin gyis der bkod pa gang byas pa/_/de dag lha mi rnams kyis sngon ma mthong /_/sum cu gsum dbang rnam rgyal khang 'dra bar/_/de ltar gling bzhi dag ni mdzes par 
[\[150b.5\]]brgyan/_/'thab bral lha dang dga' ldan 'phrul ba dang /_/gzhan 'phrul ba dang 'og min lha yi rnams/_/stobs dang shugs 'chang thams cad brtson par ni/_/byang chub snying po dam pa rgyan par byed/_/byang chub snying por gshegs pa'i bar der ni/_/'dod pa'i khams
